{"forms":[{"form":"institutions","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"The University of the South Pacific is the only internationally accredited institution of higher education in Oceania.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A long-established organization or type of organization, particularly one involved with education, public service, or charity work."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"The institution of marriage is present in many cultures but its details vary widely across them.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A custom or practice of a society or community."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"He's been in an institution since the crash.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["The building or buildings which house such an organization."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"glosses":["A mental institution."],"tags":["countable","informal","uncountable"]},{"glosses":["Any facility where people (especially those who are mentally or physically disabled or sick, or who are prisoners) are committed (confined), where their freedom to leave is restricted."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Over time, the local pub has become something of an institution.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Any long established and respected place or business."],"tags":["countable","informal","uncountable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"She's not just any old scholar; she is an institution.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A person long established in a place, position, or field."],"tags":["countable","informal","uncountable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"The institution of higher speed limits was a popular move but increased the severity of crashes.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["The act of instituting something."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"glosses":["The act by which a bishop commits a cure of souls to a priest."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"],"topics":["Christianity"]},{"glosses":["That which institutes or instructs, particularly a textbook or system of elements or rules."],"tags":["countable","obsolete","uncountable"]},{"glosses":["A correctional institution."],"tags":["countable","informal","uncountable"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˌɪn.stɪˈtʃuː.ʃən/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/ˌɪn.stɪˈtjuː.ʃən/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]},{"ipa":"/ˌɪn.stɪˈtu.ʃən/","tags":["Canada","General-American"]},{"ipa":"/ˌɪn.stɪˈtjʉː.ʃən/","tags":["Australia"]}],"synonyms":[{"word":"establishment"}],"upstream_line":258691,"upstream_offset":1287666073,"upstream_record_sha256":"5f7f4ecd0e1395e1c07ffb8fc400bd91e72019453987f035bec705e354d520d4","word":"institution"}
